## 12. Kžzd-7/2017-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2017-10-19
**Evidence:** Victim's written statement + derived witness examination record
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Victim's written statement (pages 19-20) was obtained as "informational conversation" in written form due to victim's refusal to speak orally with police. Subsequent witness examination derived from this illegally obtained statement.

### Holding
Written statement excluded as it violated **Article 78(3) ZKP/97** (informational conversation must be oral). Witness examination from April 8, 2010 also excluded under **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP/07** - fruit of poisonous tree doctrine.

---

## 13. K-31/2020-127 (Županijski sud u Splitu)

**Date:** 2021-02-23
**Evidence:** Police informational statements (službene bilješke)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police informational statements regarding circumstances of the crime were proposed as evidence.

### Holding
Evidence excluded per **Article 431(3) in conjunction with Article 86 ZKP/08**. Police informational statements cannot serve as basis for judicial decisions nor be used in proceedings.

### Principle

---

## 14. I Kž-207/2021-13 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Physician testimony
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant's physician was examined as witness without being warned of professional privilege under Article 285 ZKP.

### Holding
Physician testimony excluded. Medical professionals must be warned of their right to refuse testimony about patient information before examination.

### Key Provision
**Article 285 ZKP** - Professional privilege warning required for physicians.

---

## 15. I Kž-121/2019-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2019
**Evidence:** Telecommunication traffic data + derived evidence
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police obtained telecom traffic data without proper judicial authorization. Subsequent evidence derived from this data.

### Holding
Both primary telecom evidence and all derivative evidence excluded under fruit of poisonous tree doctrine.

### Key Provision
**Article 339 ZKP** (telecom data) + **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP** (derivative exclusion)

---

## 16. Kž-361/2023-6 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2023
**Evidence:** Spouse testimony + search evidence
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant's spouse testified without proper warning of privilege. Additionally, search witnesses were not continuously present.

### Holding
Both spouse testimony (missing Art. 285 warning) and search evidence (Art. 254(2) witness presence violation) excluded.

### Key Provisions
**Article 285 ZKP** (spousal privilege) + **Article 254(2) ZKP** (continuous witness presence)

---

## 17. I Kž-Us-30/2022-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2022
**Evidence:** Defendant's statements during questioning
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant was interrogated using witness questioning procedures rather than defendant interrogation procedures.

### Holding
Statements excluded. Defendant has distinct procedural rights that cannot be circumvented by treating them as a witness.

### Key Provision
**Article 208.a ZKP** - Defendant interrogation procedures.

---

## 18. Kž-136/2021-6 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Search report and seized items
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Search warrant issued but lacked adequate justification for the search.

### Holding
Evidence excluded due to deficient search authorization under Article 242 ZKP.

### Key Provision
**Article 242 ZKP** - Search authorization must contain adequate grounds.

---

## 19. I Kž-702/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020
**Evidence:** Items seized from home search
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted home search without written warrant.

### Holding
All evidence from warrantless search excluded per Article 240 ZKP.

### Key Provision
**Article 240 ZKP** - Written search order required.

---

## 20. Kžm-13/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020
**Evidence:** Blood sample analysis results (juvenile case)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Blood samples taken from juvenile defendant without juvenile court judge's order.

### Holding
Blood analysis excluded. Juvenile proceedings require specific judicial authorization for bodily samples.

### Key Provision
**Article 77(5) ZSM** (Zakon o sudovima za mladež) - Juvenile judge's order required.

---

## 21. I Kž 484/2011-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011
**Evidence:** Items seized during home entry
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police entered home before receiving fax copy of search warrant. Entry preceded authorization.

### Holding
Evidence excluded. Authorization must be received before entry, not after.

### Key Provisions
**Article 240 ZKP** + **Article 247 ZKP** - Warrant must precede entry.

---

## 22. I Kž-506/2011-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011
**Evidence:** Knife and hatchet seized from home
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police entered defendant's home without warrant or valid consent.

### Holding
Weapons excluded as fruit of warrantless home entry violating Article 74 ZPP.

### Key Provision
**Article 74 ZPP** (Zakon o policijskim poslovima) - Written authorization required.

---

## 23. I Kž-Us-126/2010-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2010
**Evidence:** Items seized during home entry
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police claimed consent for entry, but consent was not explicitly given or documented.

### Holding
Evidence excluded. Consent must be explicit and documented, not implied.

### Key Provision
**Article 10(2) ZKP** - Entry without valid consent violates constitutional protections.

---

## 24. Kov-10/2019-31 (Sud)

**Date:** 2019
**Evidence:** Police informational notes (službene bilješke)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police informational notes offered as evidence of defendant's statements.

### Holding
Notes excluded per Article 86(4) ZKP. Police službene bilješke cannot serve as evidence.

### Key Provision
**Article 86(4) ZKP** - Police notes inadmissible as substantive evidence.

---

## 25. I Kž-145/2021-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Police informal records
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police submitted informal records of conversations with defendant.

### Holding
Records excluded. Informal police documentation cannot substitute for proper procedural records.

### Key Provision
**Article 86 ZKP** - Police informal records inadmissible.

---

## 26. I Kž-396/2004-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2004-09-14
**Evidence:** Search protocol, forensic examination findings, photographs
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Search conducted with witnesses present non-simultaneously across different rooms. The 1998 ZKP required two witnesses, with simultaneous presence implied.

### Holding
Evidence excluded as "poisoned fruit" under **Article 331(2) ZKP** in conjunction with **Article 9(2) and 68(3) ZKP**. Witness presence requirement means simultaneous observation throughout each distinct search action.

### Key Provision
**Article 214(1) ZKP** - Two witnesses must be simultaneously present throughout search.

---

## 27. I Kž-237/1999-5 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2002-11-19
**Evidence:** Vehicle search evidence (yellow PVC bag with drug paraphernalia)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police opened and examined bag contents before obtaining formal search authorization. Warrant issued afterward.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 213(1) ZKP** and **Article 217 ZKP**. Police cannot commence search before securing necessary documentation.

### Key Provision
**Article 213(1) ZKP** - Written warrant required before search begins.

---

## 28. I Kž-23/2005-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2007-01-16
**Evidence:** Witness statements and records from K.P.
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Witness records characterized as "nezakoniti dokazi" (unlawful evidence) per prior Supreme Court decision.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 9 ZKP**. Without excluded evidence, prosecution could not establish substance identity. Acquittal resulted.

### Key Provision
**Article 9 ZKP** - Unlawful evidence provision.

---

## 29. I Kž-216/2007-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2007-03-13
**Evidence:** Police official notes (službene bilješke) from multiple police directorates
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police documentation offered as evidence basis.

### Holding
First-instance court excluded records ex officio under **Article 78 ZKP**. Appellate court upheld exclusion, exercising judicial discretion under **Article 274(4) ZKP**.

### Key Provision
**Article 78 ZKP** - Police notes cannot serve as evidence basis.

---

## 30. I Kž-360/2004-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2005-01-20
**Evidence:** Search warrant record, seizure certificates, forensic findings
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted apartment search without judicial warrant. No arrest warrant existed, crime didn't meet three-year minimum sentence threshold, no imminent danger.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 217 ZKP**. Warrantless search without meeting **Article 216(1) ZKP** statutory requirements renders all derivative evidence inadmissible.

### Key Provision
**Article 216(1) ZKP** - Statutory exceptions for warrantless search must be satisfied.

---

## 31. Kž-318/2004-3 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2004
**Evidence:** 12 grams marijuana, search records, police officer testimony, THC test results
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED (ACQUITTAL)

### Facts
Police conducted personal search without judicial warrant. Emergency conditions under Article 216(3) did not apply.

### Holding
All evidence excluded under **Articles 9(2), 213(1), 216(3), 217 ZKP**. Without lawful evidence, conviction cannot stand. Fruit of poisonous tree doctrine applied.

### Key Provision
**Article 216(3) ZKP** - Emergency search exceptions must actually apply.

---

## 32. I Kž-119/1999-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 1999
**Evidence:** Search records, seizure certificates, official notes, expert examination records, expert findings
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted warrantless house search without required witnesses present. Claimed oral citizen report as justification.

### Holding
All evidence and derivatives excluded under **Article 9(2) ZKP** and **Article 216(2) ZKP**. Search "može se poduzeti samo u nazočnosti svjedoka" (may only be undertaken in presence of witnesses).

### Key Provision
**Article 216(2) ZKP** - Witnesses required for search.

---

## 33. I Kž-492/1997-6 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 1999-01-21
**Evidence:** All items seized from attorney's office
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police searched attorney M.C.'s law office without representative from Croatian Bar Association present as required by law.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 17 of the Law on Attorneys** and **Article 9(2) ZKP**. Attorney office searches require presence of Bar Association representative regardless of whether attorney is defendant or third party.

### Key Provision
**Article 17 Zakon o odvjetništvu** - Bar Association representative mandatory during attorney office searches.

---

## 34. Kž-375/2007-3 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2007
**Evidence:** Police interrogation record, defendant statement
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police interrogated defendant without presence of defense counsel.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 78(1) ZKP**. "Policija ne smije ispitivati okrivljenika" (police may not interrogate defendant). Only proper authorities may conduct defendant interrogations with required procedural protections.

### Key Provision
**Article 78(1) ZKP** - Police interrogation of defendants prohibited.

---

## 35. I Kž-115/2005-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2008-01-29
**Evidence:** Search records and all evidence derived from bag search
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police claimed to conduct "inspection" (pregled) of defendant's bag but actually performed a search by opening and examining contents - exceeding inspection scope.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Articles 9(2), 213(1), 217 ZKP**. Opening and examining bag contents constitutes "search" requiring judicial warrant, not "inspection" under police powers.

### Key Distinction
**Inspection vs Search** - Visual observation permitted; opening closed items requires search warrant.

---

## 36. I Kž-14/2001-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2002-09-11
**Evidence:** Heroin (0.09g), toxicology expert report, seizure confirmation
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted garage search without written court order and without required witnesses present. Garage not part of apprehension location.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Articles 217, 9(2), 213, 214 ZKP**. Search of garage requires same protections as residence - warrant plus witnesses.

### Key Provision
**Article 217 ZKP** - Unlawful search records inadmissible.

---

## 37. I Kž-383/1999-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2000-03-22
**Evidence:** Police official notes about questioning defendant
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police questioned defendant and documented responses in official notes (službene bilješke).

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 177(6) ZKP** in conjunction with **Article 367(2) ZKP**. Police may not question suspects about the crime. Their notes about such questioning are inadmissible.

### Key Provision
**Article 177(6) ZKP** - Police prohibition on questioning suspects about offense.

---

## 38. I Kž-1021/2005-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2006-01-19
**Evidence:** Search record, seizure confirmations, expert report, witness statement portions, photographs (10-13, 19-32, 35-42, 47-50)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
During apartment search on 27 July 1999, the two required witnesses were not simultaneously present throughout. One witness remained in hallway and could not observe the moment contraband was discovered in the stove.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Articles 214(1), 217, 331(2), 78, 9 ZKP**. Two adult witnesses must be "simultaneously present throughout the entire duration of the search" - this is a "basic constitutive element" of lawful search procedure.

### Key Provision
**Article 214(1) ZKP** - Simultaneous continuous witness presence required.

---

## Quick Reference: Exclusion Grounds

| Ground | Article | Cases |
|--------|---------|-------|
| Warrantless home entry | Art. 74 ZPP | Kž-602/2023-4, I Kž-506/2011-4, I Kž-119/1999-3, I Kž-14/2001-3 |
| Missing witness warning | Art. 285(3) | I Kž 15/2020-4, I Kž-207/2021-13 |
| Search disguised as inspection | Art. 240-254, 213(1) | Kž-33/2017-5, I Kž-115/2005-3 |
| Disputed consent | Art. 10(2) | I Kž 439/16.-4, I Kž-Us-126/2010-4 |
| No written warrant | Art. 240, 254 | 8 Kž-314/16-4, I Kž-702/2020-4, I Kž-360/2004-3, I Kž-14/2001-3 |
| Authorization deficiencies | Art. 242 | Kzz 33/2021-3, Kž-136/2021-6 |
| Computer search without warrant | Art. 250(1)(1) | I Kž-668/2019-4 |
| Evidence derived from unlawful photo | Art. 10(2)(4) | I Kž-221/2021-4 |
| Witness not continuously present | Art. 254(2), 214(1) | Kž-409/2023-10, Kž-361/2023-6, I Kž-396/2004-3, I Kž-1021/2005-3 |
| Witness lacked physical capacity | Art. 214(1) | I Kž 581/11-4 |
| Written informational conversation | Art. 78(3), 10(2)(4) | Kžzd-7/2017-4 |
| Police informational statements | Art. 431(3), 86, 177(6) | K-31/2020-127, Kov-10/2019-31, I Kž-145/2021-4, I Kž-216/2007-3, I Kž-383/1999-3 |
| Telecom data without order | Art. 339, 10(2)(4) | I Kž-121/2019-4 |
| Spousal privilege violation | Art. 285 | Kž-361/2023-6 |
| Defendant as witness | Art. 208.a | I Kž-Us-30/2022-4 |
| Juvenile samples without order | Art. 77(5) ZSM | Kžm-13/2020-4 |
| Entry before warrant received | Art. 240, 247 | I Kž 484/2011-4, I Kž-237/1999-5 |
| Personal search without emergency | Art. 216(3), 217 | Kž-318/2004-3 |
| Search without witnesses | Art. 216(2), 214 | I Kž-119/1999-3, I Kž-14/2001-3 |
| Attorney office without Bar rep | Art. 17 Zakon o odvj. | I Kž-492/1997-6 |
| Police interrogation of defendant | Art. 78(1) | Kž-375/2007-3 |

---

*Source: Combined analysis - 810 original + 200 documents from new 557-URL dataset*
*Last updated: 2026-01-08*

<!-- COMMIT: 5db56bf38d39a0d66d17246fb2f38e565f3537ff -->
# Successful Evidence Exclusions

Cases where courts ruled evidence inadmissible under Article 10 ZKP.

---

## 1. I Kž 15/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020-02-12
**Evidence:** Privileged witness testimony (father of defendant) + search report
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Court failed to properly record the statutory warning required by Article 285(3) ZKP when examining a privileged witness (defendant's father).

### Holding
Testimony excluded under Article 10(2)(3) ZKP because the minutes did not document:
- The statutory warning given
- The witness's response to the warning

### Key Provision
**Article 285(3) ZKP** - Privileged witnesses must be warned of their right to refuse testimony, and this warning + response must be recorded.

---

## 2. Kž-602/2023-4 (Županijski sud u Osijeku)

**Date:** 2024-02-01
**Evidence:** All items seized from apartment + forensic reports
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police officers entered defendant's apartment without:
- Written warrant
- Resident invitation
- Emergency circumstances

### Holding
All evidence excluded. Entry violated **Article 74 of the Law on Police Duties** (Zakon o policijskim poslovima).

### Derivative Evidence
Forensic examination records, toxicological reports, and photo documentation also excluded as fruit of the poisonous tree.

---

## 3. Kž-33/2017-5 (Županijski sud u Bjelovaru)

**Date:** 2017-02-23
**Evidence:** On-site inspection records, seizure records, photographs
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted what they labeled an "on-site inspection" (očevid) at defendant's home, but actually performed a search without proper authorization.

### Holding
Court found this was a **home search disguised as an inspection**:
- No formal search warrant obtained
- Defendant's "request" for police entry was not genuine
- Witness presence requirements violated

### Key Distinction
**Inspection (očevid)** vs **Search (pretraga)** - inspections at crime scenes don't require warrants, but entering a home to find evidence requires full search protections.

---

## 4. I Kž 439/16.-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2016-08-30
**Evidence:** Documents from home entry, seizure confirmations
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police claimed defendant consented to entry. Defendant alleged coercion/threats.

### Holding
Factual circumstances **insufficiently established**:
- Lower court didn't adequately examine consent validity
- Allegations of threats contradicted conclusions
- Evidence excluded until facts properly determined through witness examination

### Principle
**Consent must be proven genuine** - disputed consent requires factual inquiry before evidence can be admitted.

---

## 5. 8 Kž-314/16-4 (Županijski sud u Varaždinu)

**Date:** 2016-09-14
**Evidence:** 9 kg finely cut tobacco
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted search of defendant's home without:
- Written warrant
- Required witnesses present

### Holding
Search violated **Articles 240 and 254 ZKP**:
- Art. 240: Written search order required
- Art. 254: Two witnesses must be present during search

Items seized as product of unlawful search cannot be used per **Article 250 ZKP**.

---

## 6. Kž-234/2023-7 (Županijski sud u Osijeku)

**Date:** 2023-09-01
**Evidence:** Search report containing statement and police officer testimony
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Prior appellate decision (Kž-523/2021-4) had already ruled portions of search report unlawful.

### Holding
First-instance court properly identified and excluded:
- V.D.'s statement contained in search report
- Police officer L.D.'s testimony

### Principle
**Prior exclusion rulings bind subsequent proceedings** - once evidence ruled unlawful, it remains excluded.

---

## 7. Kzz 33/2021-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021-10-19
**Evidence:** Search records, toxicological reports
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Deficiencies in search authorization documentation.

### Holding
Search authorization failed to meet **Article 242 ZKP** requirements. All derivative evidence (toxicological analysis) also excluded.

---

## 8. I Kž-668/2019-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020-04-20
**Evidence:** IT expert report and opinion (pages 1292-1304)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Computer search conducted during expert analysis without written search warrant. The search authorization only covered expert analysis, not the search itself.

### Holding
Expert report excluded under **Article 10(2)(3) ZKP/08** and **Article 250(1)(1) ZKP/08** because the underlying computer search lacked proper judicial order.

### Key Distinction
**Expert authorization ≠ Search authorization** - permission to analyze a device does not include permission to search it.

---

## 9. I Kž-221/2021-4 (Visoki kazneni sud)

**Date:** 2021-12-14
**Evidence:** Portion of forensic medical expert report (knife wound mechanism conclusions)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Expert's findings were based on photograph 16 from crime scene documentation. That photograph resulted from an unlawfully conducted search of defendant's summer kitchen.

### Holding
Under **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP/08**, the expert opinion derived from unlawful evidence is also unlawful (fruit of poisonous tree).

### Principle
**Derivative evidence exclusion** - even expert conclusions based on unlawfully obtained photos must be excluded.

---

## 10. Kž-409/2023-10 (Županijski sud u Puli)

**Date:** 2024-02-29
**Evidence:** Search record, seizure confirmations, toxicological expert report
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Home search conducted without **simultaneous presence of two citizen witnesses** as required by law. Witness K.M. remained in one room throughout, unable to observe police actions or moment of evidence discovery.

### Holding
Evidence excluded per **Article 250(7) ZKP/08** - unlawful search renders evidence inadmissible. Witnesses must be capable of continuously monitoring police conduct.

### Principle
**Continuous witness presence required** - static witnesses who cannot observe discovery of evidence fail Art. 254(2) requirements.

---

## 11. I Kž 581/11-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011-09-21
**Evidence:** Search record and confirmation of temporary seizure
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
The search witness was a person with **100% physical disability (blind)** who could not objectively perform the functional duties required by law.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 9(2) ZKP** and **Article 214(1) ZKP**. Despite no objections being raised at trial, the blind witness lacked actual capacity to fulfill witness obligations under procedural rules.

### Principle
**Witness capacity requirement** - witnesses must be physically capable of observing search activities. Formal presence without functional capacity is insufficient.

---

## 12. Kžzd-7/2017-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2017-10-19
**Evidence:** Victim's written statement + derived witness examination record
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Victim's written statement (pages 19-20) was obtained as "informational conversation" in written form due to victim's refusal to speak orally with police. Subsequent witness examination derived from this illegally obtained statement.

### Holding
Written statement excluded as it violated **Article 78(3) ZKP/97** (informational conversation must be oral). Witness examination from April 8, 2010 also excluded under **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP/07** - fruit of poisonous tree doctrine.

### Principle
**Informational conversations must be oral** - written "informational conversations" are unlawful, and all derivative evidence must also be excluded.

---

## 13. K-31/2020-127 (Županijski sud u Splitu)

**Date:** 2021-02-23
**Evidence:** Police informational statements (službene bilješke)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police informational statements regarding circumstances of the crime were proposed as evidence.

### Holding
Evidence excluded per **Article 431(3) in conjunction with Article 86 ZKP/08**. Police informational statements cannot serve as basis for judicial decisions nor be used in proceedings.

### Principle
**Police bilješke are not evidence** - informational notes cannot be used as substantive evidence in criminal proceedings.

---

## 14. I Kž-207/2021-13 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Physician testimony
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant's physician was examined as witness without being warned of professional privilege under Article 285 ZKP.

### Holding
Physician testimony excluded. Medical professionals must be warned of their right to refuse testimony about patient information before examination.

### Key Provision
**Article 285 ZKP** - Professional privilege warning required for physicians.

---

## 15. I Kž-121/2019-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2019
**Evidence:** Telecommunication traffic data + derived evidence
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police obtained telecom traffic data without proper judicial authorization. Subsequent evidence derived from this data.

### Holding
Both primary telecom evidence and all derivative evidence excluded under fruit of poisonous tree doctrine.

### Key Provision
**Article 339 ZKP** (telecom data) + **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP** (derivative exclusion)

---

## 16. Kž-361/2023-6 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2023
**Evidence:** Spouse testimony + search evidence
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant's spouse testified without proper warning of privilege. Additionally, search witnesses were not continuously present.

### Holding
Both spouse testimony (missing Art. 285 warning) and search evidence (Art. 254(2) witness presence violation) excluded.

### Key Provisions
**Article 285 ZKP** (spousal privilege) + **Article 254(2) ZKP** (continuous witness presence)

---

## 17. I Kž-Us-30/2022-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2022
**Evidence:** Defendant's statements during questioning
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant was interrogated using witness questioning procedures rather than defendant interrogation procedures.

### Holding
Statements excluded. Defendant has distinct procedural rights that cannot be circumvented by treating them as a witness.

### Key Provision
**Article 208.a ZKP** - Defendant interrogation procedures.

---

## 18. Kž-136/2021-6 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Search report and seized items
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Search warrant issued but lacked adequate justification for the search.

### Holding
Evidence excluded due to deficient search authorization under Article 242 ZKP.

### Key Provision
**Article 242 ZKP** - Search authorization must contain adequate grounds.

## 19. I Kž-702/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020
**Evidence:** Items seized from home search
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted home search without written warrant.

### Holding
All evidence from warrantless search excluded per Article 240 ZKP.

---

## 20. Kžm-13/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020
**Evidence:** Blood sample analysis results (juvenile case)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Blood samples taken from juvenile defendant without juvenile court judge's order.

### Holding
Blood analysis excluded. Juvenile proceedings require specific judicial authorization for bodily samples.

## 21. I Kž 484/2011-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011
**Evidence:** Items seized during home entry
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police entered home before receiving fax copy of search warrant. Entry preceded authorization.

### Holding
Evidence excluded. Authorization must be received before entry, not after.

### Key Provisions
**Article 240 ZKP** + **Article 247 ZKP** - Warrant must precede entry.

## 22. I Kž-506/2011-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011
**Evidence:** Knife and hatchet seized from home
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police entered defendant's home without warrant or valid consent.

Weapons excluded as fruit of warrantless home entry violating Article 74 ZPP.

**Article 74 ZPP** (Zakon o policijskim poslovima) - Written authorization required.

**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

Police claimed consent for entry, but consent was not explicitly given or documented.

**Date:** 2019
**Evidence:** Police informational notes (službene bilješke)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

Police informational notes offered as evidence of defendant's statements.

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Police informal records
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police submitted informal records of conversations with defendant.

### Holding
Records excluded. Informal police documentation cannot substitute for proper procedural records.

---

## 26. I Kž-396/2004-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2004-09-14
**Evidence:** Search protocol, forensic examination findings, photographs
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Search conducted with witnesses present non-simultaneously across different rooms. The 1998 ZKP required two witnesses, with simultaneous presence implied.

### Holding
Evidence excluded as "poisoned fruit" under **Article 331(2) ZKP** in conjunction with **Article 9(2) and 68(3) ZKP**. Witness presence requirement means simultaneous observation throughout each distinct search action.

### Key Provision

---

## 27. I Kž-237/1999-5 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2002-11-19
**Evidence:** Vehicle search evidence (yellow PVC bag with drug paraphernalia)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police opened and examined bag contents before obtaining formal search authorization. Warrant issued afterward.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 213(1) ZKP** and **Article 217 ZKP**. Police cannot commence search before securing necessary documentation.

### Key Provision
**Article 213(1) ZKP** - Written warrant required before search begins.

---

## 28. I Kž-23/2005-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2007-01-16
**Evidence:** Witness statements and records from K.P.
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Witness records characterized as "nezakoniti dokazi" (unlawful evidence) per prior Supreme Court decision.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 9 ZKP**. Without excluded evidence, prosecution could not establish substance identity. Acquittal resulted.

### Key Provision
**Article 9 ZKP** - Unlawful evidence provision.

---

## 29. I Kž-216/2007-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2007-03-13
**Evidence:** Police official notes (službene bilješke) from multiple police directorates
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police documentation offered as evidence basis.

### Holding
First-instance court excluded records ex officio under **Article 78 ZKP**. Appellate court upheld exclusion, exercising judicial discretion under **Article 274(4) ZKP**.

### Key Provision
**Article 78 ZKP** - Police notes cannot serve as evidence basis.

---

## 30. I Kž-360/2004-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2005-01-20
**Evidence:** Search warrant record, seizure certificates, forensic findings
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted apartment search without judicial warrant. No arrest warrant existed, crime didn't meet three-year minimum sentence threshold, no imminent danger.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 217 ZKP**. Warrantless search without meeting **Article 216(1) ZKP** statutory requirements renders all derivative evidence inadmissible.

### Key Provision
**Article 216(1) ZKP** - Statutory exceptions for warrantless search must be satisfied.

---

## 31. Kž-318/2004-3 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2004
**Evidence:** 12 grams marijuana, search records, police officer testimony, THC test results
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED (ACQUITTAL)

### Facts
Police conducted personal search without judicial warrant. Emergency conditions under Article 216(3) did not apply.

### Holding
All evidence excluded under **Articles 9(2), 213(1), 216(3), 217 ZKP**. Without lawful evidence, conviction cannot stand. Fruit of poisonous tree doctrine applied.

### Key Provision
**Article 216(3) ZKP** - Emergency search exceptions must actually apply.

---

## 32. I Kž-119/1999-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 1999
**Evidence:** Search records, seizure certificates, official notes, expert examination records, expert findings
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted warrantless house search without required witnesses present. Claimed oral citizen report as justification.

### Holding
All evidence and derivatives excluded under **Article 9(2) ZKP** and **Article 216(2) ZKP**. Search "može se poduzeti samo u nazočnosti svjedoka" (may only be undertaken in presence of witnesses).

### Key Provision
**Article 216(2) ZKP** - Witnesses required for search.

---

## Quick Reference: Exclusion Grounds

| Ground | Article | Cases |
|--------|---------|-------|
| Warrantless home entry | Art. 74 ZPP | Kž-602/2023-4, I Kž-506/2011-4, I Kž-119/1999-3 |
| Missing witness warning | Art. 285(3) | I Kž 15/2020-4, I Kž-207/2021-13 |
| Search disguised as inspection | Art. 240-254 | Kž-33/2017-5 |
| Disputed consent | Art. 10(2) | I Kž 439/16.-4, I Kž-Us-126/2010-4 |
| No written warrant | Art. 240, 254 | 8 Kž-314/16-4, I Kž-702/2020-4, I Kž-360/2004-3 |
| Authorization deficiencies | Art. 242 | Kzz 33/2021-3, Kž-136/2021-6 |
| Computer search without warrant | Art. 250(1)(1) | I Kž-668/2019-4 |
| Evidence derived from unlawful photo | Art. 10(2)(4) | I Kž-221/2021-4 |
| Witness not continuously present | Art. 254(2), 250(7) | Kž-409/2023-10, Kž-361/2023-6, I Kž-396/2004-3 |
| Witness lacked physical capacity | Art. 214(1) | I Kž 581/11-4 |
| Written informational conversation | Art. 78(3), 10(2)(4) | Kžzd-7/2017-4 |
| Police informational statements | Art. 431(3), 86 | K-31/2020-127, Kov-10/2019-31, I Kž-145/2021-4, I Kž-216/2007-3 |
| Telecom data without order | Art. 339, 10(2)(4) | I Kž-121/2019-4 |
| Spousal privilege violation | Art. 285 | Kž-361/2023-6 |
| Defendant as witness | Art. 208.a | I Kž-Us-30/2022-4 |
| Juvenile samples without order | Art. 77(5) ZSM | Kžm-13/2020-4 |
| Entry before warrant received | Art. 240, 247 | I Kž 484/2011-4, I Kž-237/1999-5 |
| Personal search without emergency | Art. 216(3), 217 | Kž-318/2004-3 |
| Search without witnesses | Art. 216(2) | I Kž-119/1999-3 |

---

*Source: Combined analysis - 810 + 100 documents from new dataset*
*Last updated: 2026-01-08*

<!-- COMMIT: 7aef260eba6e2cbcc0e3ea39678d1a080459db6f -->
# Successful Evidence Exclusions

Cases where courts ruled evidence inadmissible under Article 10 ZKP.

---

## 1. I Kž 15/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020-02-12
**Evidence:** Privileged witness testimony (father of defendant) + search report
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Court failed to properly record the statutory warning required by Article 285(3) ZKP when examining a privileged witness (defendant's father).

### Holding
Testimony excluded under Article 10(2)(3) ZKP because the minutes did not document:
- The statutory warning given
- The witness's response to the warning

### Key Provision
**Article 285(3) ZKP** - Privileged witnesses must be warned of their right to refuse testimony, and this warning + response must be recorded.

---

## 2. Kž-602/2023-4 (Županijski sud u Osijeku)

**Date:** 2024-02-01
**Evidence:** All items seized from apartment + forensic reports
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police officers entered defendant's apartment without:
- Written warrant
- Resident invitation
- Emergency circumstances

### Holding
All evidence excluded. Entry violated **Article 74 of the Law on Police Duties** (Zakon o policijskim poslovima).

### Derivative Evidence
Forensic examination records, toxicological reports, and photo documentation also excluded as fruit of the poisonous tree.

---

## 3. Kž-33/2017-5 (Županijski sud u Bjelovaru)

**Date:** 2017-02-23
**Evidence:** On-site inspection records, seizure records, photographs
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted what they labeled an "on-site inspection" (očevid) at defendant's home, but actually performed a search without proper authorization.

### Holding
Court found this was a **home search disguised as an inspection**:
- No formal search warrant obtained
- Defendant's "request" for police entry was not genuine
- Witness presence requirements violated

### Key Distinction
**Inspection (očevid)** vs **Search (pretraga)** - inspections at crime scenes don't require warrants, but entering a home to find evidence requires full search protections.

---

## 4. I Kž 439/16.-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2016-08-30
**Evidence:** Documents from home entry, seizure confirmations
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police claimed defendant consented to entry. Defendant alleged coercion/threats.

### Holding
Factual circumstances **insufficiently established**:
- Lower court didn't adequately examine consent validity
- Allegations of threats contradicted conclusions
- Evidence excluded until facts properly determined through witness examination

### Principle
**Consent must be proven genuine** - disputed consent requires factual inquiry before evidence can be admitted.

---

## 5. 8 Kž-314/16-4 (Županijski sud u Varaždinu)

**Date:** 2016-09-14
**Evidence:** 9 kg finely cut tobacco
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted search of defendant's home without:
- Written warrant
- Required witnesses present

### Holding
Search violated **Articles 240 and 254 ZKP**:
- Art. 240: Written search order required
- Art. 254: Two witnesses must be present during search

Items seized as product of unlawful search cannot be used per **Article 250 ZKP**.

---

## 6. Kž-234/2023-7 (Županijski sud u Osijeku)

**Date:** 2023-09-01
**Evidence:** Search report containing statement and police officer testimony
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Prior appellate decision (Kž-523/2021-4) had already ruled portions of search report unlawful.

### Holding
First-instance court properly identified and excluded:
- V.D.'s statement contained in search report
- Police officer L.D.'s testimony

### Principle
**Prior exclusion rulings bind subsequent proceedings** - once evidence ruled unlawful, it remains excluded.

---

## 7. Kzz 33/2021-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021-10-19
**Evidence:** Search records, toxicological reports
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Deficiencies in search authorization documentation.

### Holding
Search authorization failed to meet **Article 242 ZKP** requirements. All derivative evidence (toxicological analysis) also excluded.

---

## 8. I Kž-668/2019-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020-04-20
**Evidence:** IT expert report and opinion (pages 1292-1304)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Computer search conducted during expert analysis without written search warrant. The search authorization only covered expert analysis, not the search itself.

### Holding
Expert report excluded under **Article 10(2)(3) ZKP/08** and **Article 250(1)(1) ZKP/08** because the underlying computer search lacked proper judicial order.

### Key Distinction
**Expert authorization ≠ Search authorization** - permission to analyze a device does not include permission to search it.

---

## 9. I Kž-221/2021-4 (Visoki kazneni sud)

**Date:** 2021-12-14
**Evidence:** Portion of forensic medical expert report (knife wound mechanism conclusions)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Expert's findings were based on photograph 16 from crime scene documentation. That photograph resulted from an unlawfully conducted search of defendant's summer kitchen.

### Holding
Under **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP/08**, the expert opinion derived from unlawful evidence is also unlawful (fruit of poisonous tree).

### Principle
**Derivative evidence exclusion** - even expert conclusions based on unlawfully obtained photos must be excluded.

---

## 10. Kž-409/2023-10 (Županijski sud u Puli)

**Date:** 2024-02-29
**Evidence:** Search record, seizure confirmations, toxicological expert report
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Home search conducted without **simultaneous presence of two citizen witnesses** as required by law. Witness K.M. remained in one room throughout, unable to observe police actions or moment of evidence discovery.

### Holding
Evidence excluded per **Article 250(7) ZKP/08** - unlawful search renders evidence inadmissible. Witnesses must be capable of continuously monitoring police conduct.

### Principle
**Continuous witness presence required** - static witnesses who cannot observe discovery of evidence fail Art. 254(2) requirements.

---

## 11. I Kž 581/11-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011-09-21
**Evidence:** Search record and confirmation of temporary seizure
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
The search witness was a person with **100% physical disability (blind)** who could not objectively perform the functional duties required by law.

### Holding
Evidence excluded under **Article 9(2) ZKP** and **Article 214(1) ZKP**. Despite no objections being raised at trial, the blind witness lacked actual capacity to fulfill witness obligations under procedural rules.

### Principle
**Witness capacity requirement** - witnesses must be physically capable of observing search activities. Formal presence without functional capacity is insufficient.

---

## 12. Kžzd-7/2017-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2017-10-19
**Evidence:** Victim's written statement + derived witness examination record
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Victim's written statement (pages 19-20) was obtained as "informational conversation" in written form due to victim's refusal to speak orally with police. Subsequent witness examination derived from this illegally obtained statement.

### Holding
Written statement excluded as it violated **Article 78(3) ZKP/97** (informational conversation must be oral). Witness examination from April 8, 2010 also excluded under **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP/07** - fruit of poisonous tree doctrine.

### Principle
**Informational conversations must be oral** - written "informational conversations" are unlawful, and all derivative evidence must also be excluded.

---

## 13. K-31/2020-127 (Županijski sud u Splitu)

**Date:** 2021-02-23
**Evidence:** Police informational statements (službene bilješke)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police informational statements regarding circumstances of the crime were proposed as evidence.

### Holding
Evidence excluded per **Article 431(3) in conjunction with Article 86 ZKP/08**. Police informational statements cannot serve as basis for judicial decisions nor be used in proceedings.

### Principle
**Police bilješke are not evidence** - informational notes cannot be used as substantive evidence in criminal proceedings.

---

## 14. I Kž-207/2021-13 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Physician testimony
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant's physician was examined as witness without being warned of professional privilege under Article 285 ZKP.

### Holding
Physician testimony excluded. Medical professionals must be warned of their right to refuse testimony about patient information before examination.

### Key Provision
**Article 285 ZKP** - Professional privilege warning required for physicians.

---

## 15. I Kž-121/2019-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2019
**Evidence:** Telecommunication traffic data + derived evidence
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police obtained telecom traffic data without proper judicial authorization. Subsequent evidence derived from this data.

### Holding
Both primary telecom evidence and all derivative evidence excluded under fruit of poisonous tree doctrine.

### Key Provision
**Article 339 ZKP** (telecom data) + **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP** (derivative exclusion)

---

## 16. Kž-361/2023-6 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2023
**Evidence:** Spouse testimony + search evidence
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant's spouse testified without proper warning of privilege. Additionally, search witnesses were not continuously present.

### Holding
Both spouse testimony (missing Art. 285 warning) and search evidence (Art. 254(2) witness presence violation) excluded.

### Key Provisions
**Article 285 ZKP** (spousal privilege) + **Article 254(2) ZKP** (continuous witness presence)

---

## 17. I Kž-Us-30/2022-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2022
**Evidence:** Defendant's statements during questioning
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant was interrogated using witness questioning procedures rather than defendant interrogation procedures.

### Holding
Statements excluded. Defendant has distinct procedural rights that cannot be circumvented by treating them as a witness.

### Key Provision
**Article 208.a ZKP** - Defendant interrogation procedures.

---

## 18. Kž-136/2021-6 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Search report and seized items
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Search warrant issued but lacked adequate justification for the search.

### Holding
Evidence excluded due to deficient search authorization under Article 242 ZKP.

### Key Provision
**Article 242 ZKP** - Search authorization must contain adequate grounds.

---

## 19. I Kž-702/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020
**Evidence:** Items seized from home search
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted home search without written warrant.

### Holding
All evidence from warrantless search excluded per Article 240 ZKP.

### Key Provision
**Article 240 ZKP** - Written search order required.

---

## 20. Kžm-13/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020
**Evidence:** Blood sample analysis results (juvenile case)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Blood samples taken from juvenile defendant without juvenile court judge's order.

### Holding
Blood analysis excluded. Juvenile proceedings require specific judicial authorization for bodily samples.

### Key Provision
**Article 77(5) ZSM** (Zakon o sudovima za mladež) - Juvenile judge's order required.

---

## 21. I Kž 484/2011-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011
**Evidence:** Items seized during home entry
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police entered home before receiving fax copy of search warrant. Entry preceded authorization.

### Holding
Evidence excluded. Authorization must be received before entry, not after.

### Key Provisions
**Article 240 ZKP** + **Article 247 ZKP** - Warrant must precede entry.

---

## 22. I Kž-506/2011-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011
**Evidence:** Knife and hatchet seized from home
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police entered defendant's home without warrant or valid consent.

### Holding
Weapons excluded as fruit of warrantless home entry violating Article 74 ZPP.

### Key Provision
**Article 74 ZPP** (Zakon o policijskim poslovima) - Written authorization required.

---

## 23. I Kž-Us-126/2010-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2010
**Evidence:** Items seized during home entry
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police claimed consent for entry, but consent was not explicitly given or documented.

### Holding
Evidence excluded. Consent must be explicit and documented, not implied.

### Key Provision
**Article 10(2) ZKP** - Entry without valid consent violates constitutional protections.

---

## 24. Kov-10/2019-31 (Sud)

**Date:** 2019
**Evidence:** Police informational notes (službene bilješke)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police informational notes offered as evidence of defendant's statements.

### Holding
Notes excluded per Article 86(4) ZKP. Police službene bilješke cannot serve as evidence.

### Key Provision
**Article 86(4) ZKP** - Police notes inadmissible as substantive evidence.

---

## 25. I Kž-145/2021-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Police informal records
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police submitted informal records of conversations with defendant.

### Holding
Records excluded. Informal police documentation cannot substitute for proper procedural records.

### Key Provision
**Article 86 ZKP** - Police informal records inadmissible.

---

## Quick Reference: Exclusion Grounds

| Ground | Article | Cases |
|--------|---------|-------|
| Warrantless home entry | Art. 74 ZPP | Kž-602/2023-4, I Kž-506/2011-4 |
| Missing witness warning | Art. 285(3) | I Kž 15/2020-4, I Kž-207/2021-13 |
| Search disguised as inspection | Art. 240-254 | Kž-33/2017-5 |
| Disputed consent | Art. 10(2) | I Kž 439/16.-4, I Kž-Us-126/2010-4 |
| No written warrant | Art. 240, 254 | 8 Kž-314/16-4, I Kž-702/2020-4 |
| Authorization deficiencies | Art. 242 | Kzz 33/2021-3, Kž-136/2021-6 |
| Computer search without warrant | Art. 250(1)(1) | I Kž-668/2019-4 |
| Evidence derived from unlawful photo | Art. 10(2)(4) | I Kž-221/2021-4 |
| Witness not continuously present | Art. 254(2), 250(7) | Kž-409/2023-10, Kž-361/2023-6 |
| Witness lacked physical capacity | Art. 214(1) | I Kž 581/11-4 |
| Written informational conversation | Art. 78(3), 10(2)(4) | Kžzd-7/2017-4 |
| Police informational statements | Art. 431(3), 86 | K-31/2020-127, Kov-10/2019-31, I Kž-145/2021-4 |
| Telecom data without order | Art. 339, 10(2)(4) | I Kž-121/2019-4 |
| Spousal privilege violation | Art. 285 | Kž-361/2023-6 |
| Defendant as witness | Art. 208.a | I Kž-Us-30/2022-4 |
| Juvenile samples without order | Art. 77(5) ZSM | Kžm-13/2020-4 |
| Entry before warrant received | Art. 240, 247 | I Kž 484/2011-4 |

---

## 15. I Kž-121/2019-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2019
**Evidence:** Telecommunication traffic data + derived evidence
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police obtained telecom traffic data without proper judicial authorization. Subsequent evidence derived from this data.

### Holding
Both primary telecom evidence and all derivative evidence excluded under fruit of poisonous tree doctrine.

### Key Provision
**Article 339 ZKP** (telecom data) + **Article 10(2)(4) ZKP** (derivative exclusion)

---

## 16. Kž-361/2023-6 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2023
**Evidence:** Spouse testimony + search evidence
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant's spouse testified without proper warning of privilege. Additionally, search witnesses were not continuously present.

### Holding
Both spouse testimony (missing Art. 285 warning) and search evidence (Art. 254(2) witness presence violation) excluded.

### Key Provisions
**Article 285 ZKP** (spousal privilege) + **Article 254(2) ZKP** (continuous witness presence)

---

## 17. I Kž-Us-30/2022-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2022
**Evidence:** Defendant's statements during questioning
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Defendant was interrogated using witness questioning procedures rather than defendant interrogation procedures.

### Holding
Statements excluded. Defendant has distinct procedural rights that cannot be circumvented by treating them as a witness.

### Key Provision
**Article 208.a ZKP** - Defendant interrogation procedures.

---

## 18. Kž-136/2021-6 (Županijski sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Search report and seized items
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Search warrant issued but lacked adequate justification for the search.

### Holding
Evidence excluded due to deficient search authorization under Article 242 ZKP.

### Key Provision
**Article 242 ZKP** - Search authorization must contain adequate grounds.

---

## 19. I Kž-702/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020
**Evidence:** Items seized from home search
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted home search without written warrant.

### Holding
All evidence from warrantless search excluded per Article 240 ZKP.

### Key Provision
**Article 240 ZKP** - Written search order required.

---

## 20. Kžm-13/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020
**Evidence:** Blood sample analysis results (juvenile case)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Blood samples taken from juvenile defendant without juvenile court judge's order.

### Holding
Blood analysis excluded. Juvenile proceedings require specific judicial authorization for bodily samples.

### Key Provision
**Article 77(5) ZSM** (Zakon o sudovima za mladež) - Juvenile judge's order required.

---

## 21. I Kž 484/2011-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011
**Evidence:** Items seized during home entry
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police entered home before receiving fax copy of search warrant. Entry preceded authorization.

### Holding
Evidence excluded. Authorization must be received before entry, not after.

### Key Provisions
**Article 240 ZKP** + **Article 247 ZKP** - Warrant must precede entry.

---

## 22. I Kž-506/2011-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2011
**Evidence:** Knife and hatchet seized from home
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police entered defendant's home without warrant or valid consent.

### Holding
Weapons excluded as fruit of warrantless home entry violating Article 74 ZPP.

### Key Provision
**Article 74 ZPP** (Zakon o policijskim poslovima) - Written authorization required.

---

## 23. I Kž-Us-126/2010-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2010
**Evidence:** Items seized during home entry
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police claimed consent for entry, but consent was not explicitly given or documented.

### Holding
Evidence excluded. Consent must be explicit and documented, not implied.

### Key Provision
**Article 10(2) ZKP** - Entry without valid consent violates constitutional protections.

---

## 24. Kov-10/2019-31 (Sud)

**Date:** 2019
**Evidence:** Police informational notes (službene bilješke)
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police informational notes offered as evidence of defendant's statements.

### Holding
Notes excluded per Article 86(4) ZKP. Police službene bilješke cannot serve as evidence.

### Key Provision
**Article 86(4) ZKP** - Police notes inadmissible as substantive evidence.

---

## 25. I Kž-145/2021-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021
**Evidence:** Police informal records
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police submitted informal records of conversations with defendant.

### Holding
Records excluded. Informal police documentation cannot substitute for proper procedural records.

### Key Provision
**Article 86 ZKP** - Police informal records inadmissible.

---

<!-- COMMIT: 71530267247ed918fcf9ef3c4cac92673684a5ae -->
# Successful Evidence Exclusions

Cases where courts ruled evidence inadmissible under Article 10 ZKP.

---

## 1. I Kž 15/2020-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2020-02-12
**Evidence:** Privileged witness testimony (father of defendant) + search report
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Court failed to properly record the statutory warning required by Article 285(3) ZKP when examining a privileged witness (defendant's father).

### Holding
Testimony excluded under Article 10(2)(3) ZKP because the minutes did not document:
- The statutory warning given
- The witness's response to the warning

### Key Provision
**Article 285(3) ZKP** - Privileged witnesses must be warned of their right to refuse testimony, and this warning + response must be recorded.

---

## 2. Kž-602/2023-4 (Županijski sud u Osijeku)

**Date:** 2024-02-01
**Evidence:** All items seized from apartment + forensic reports
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police officers entered defendant's apartment without:
- Written warrant
- Resident invitation
- Emergency circumstances

### Holding
All evidence excluded. Entry violated **Article 74 of the Law on Police Duties** (Zakon o policijskim poslovima).

### Derivative Evidence
Forensic examination records, toxicological reports, and photo documentation also excluded as fruit of the poisonous tree.

---

## 3. Kž-33/2017-5 (Županijski sud u Bjelovaru)

**Date:** 2017-02-23
**Evidence:** On-site inspection records, seizure records, photographs
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted what they labeled an "on-site inspection" (očevid) at defendant's home, but actually performed a search without proper authorization.

### Holding
Court found this was a **home search disguised as an inspection**:
- No formal search warrant obtained
- Defendant's "request" for police entry was not genuine
- Witness presence requirements violated

### Key Distinction
**Inspection (očevid)** vs **Search (pretraga)** - inspections at crime scenes don't require warrants, but entering a home to find evidence requires full search protections.

---

## 4. I Kž 439/16.-4 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2016-08-30
**Evidence:** Documents from home entry, seizure confirmations
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police claimed defendant consented to entry. Defendant alleged coercion/threats.

### Holding
Factual circumstances **insufficiently established**:
- Lower court didn't adequately examine consent validity
- Allegations of threats contradicted conclusions
- Evidence excluded until facts properly determined through witness examination

### Principle
**Consent must be proven genuine** - disputed consent requires factual inquiry before evidence can be admitted.

---

## 5. 8 Kž-314/16-4 (Županijski sud u Varaždinu)

**Date:** 2016-09-14
**Evidence:** 9 kg finely cut tobacco
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Police conducted search of defendant's home without:
- Written warrant
- Required witnesses present

### Holding
Search violated **Articles 240 and 254 ZKP**:
- Art. 240: Written search order required
- Art. 254: Two witnesses must be present during search

Items seized as product of unlawful search cannot be used per **Article 250 ZKP**.

---

## 6. Kž-234/2023-7 (Županijski sud u Osijeku)

**Date:** 2023-09-01
**Evidence:** Search report containing statement and police officer testimony
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Prior appellate decision (Kž-523/2021-4) had already ruled portions of search report unlawful.

### Holding
First-instance court properly identified and excluded:
- V.D.'s statement contained in search report
- Police officer L.D.'s testimony

### Principle
**Prior exclusion rulings bind subsequent proceedings** - once evidence ruled unlawful, it remains excluded.

---

## 7. Kzz 33/2021-3 (Vrhovni sud)

**Date:** 2021-10-19
**Evidence:** Search records, toxicological reports
**Outcome:** EXCLUDED

### Facts
Deficiencies in search authorization documentation.

### Holding
Search authorization failed to meet **Article 242 ZKP** requirements. All derivative evidence (toxicological analysis) also excluded.

---
